- **mine**: Find the work this repository keeps repeating - the same files changed the same way, job after job - and turn one of those into a draft skill. Reads git history only and sends nothing anywhere until you ask for a draft; the draft goes to /handbook:review, never straight into the repository. Use when somebody wants to know what their codebase already does over and over, or wants a skill written out of work that has already happened rather than out of the session they are in. Triggers: "what does this repo do over and over", "find the repeating work in our history", "we keep doing this same tas… ([AhmetErenLapanta/TeamHandbook](https://github.com/AhmetErenLapanta/TeamHandbook/blob/HEAD/commands/mine.md) · ★6)
